29441:   function buildTree(ds){var by={},root=[];(ds||[]).forEach(function(x){by[x.id]={id:x.id,name:x.name,code:x.code,parent:x.parent_department_id,manager:x.manager_employee_id,children:[]}});Object.keys(by).forEach(function(k){var x=by[k];if(x.parent&&by[x.parent])by[x.parent].children.push(x);else root.push(x)});function node(x,depth){var manager=H.employees.filter(function(e){return e.id===x.manager})[0];return '<div class="mr-'+Math.min(depth*3,12)+' rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-4 bg-white shadow-sm"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><div class="font-black">'+esc(x.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500">'+esc(x.code||'-')+(manager?' · مدير: '+esc(manager.name):'')+'</div></div>'+badge(x.children.length+' فرعي','info')+'</div>'+(x.children.length?'<div class="mt-3 space-y-3 border-r-2 border-slate-100 pr-4">'+x.children.map(function(c){return node(c,depth+1)}).join('')+'</div>':'')+'</div>'}return root.map(function(x){return node(x,0)}).join('')||'<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لم تُنشأ إدارات بعد</div>'}

29428: function closeModal(){var r=E('rw-hr-modal-root');if(r)r.remove()}
29429:   function ppl(){return H.employees.filter(function(e){return String(e.role||'').toLowerCase()!=='owner'&&e.role!=='مالك'}).map(function(e){return{value:e.id,label:(e.name||e.email)+' — '+e.email}})}
29430:   async function loadPeople(){var d=await q('employees');H.employees=d.rows||[];return H.employees}
29431:   async function loadBranches(){var r=await supabase.from('branches').select('id,branch_code,name,is_active').eq('company_id',H.companyId).order('name');if(r.error)throw r.error;H.branches=r.data||[];return H.branches}
29432:   function branches(){return H.branches.filter(function(x){return x.is_active!==false}).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:(x.branch_code||'')+' — '+x.name}})}
29433:   function employeeOpts(){return ppl()}
29434:   function deptOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.name}})}
29435:   function posOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.title}})}
29436:   function scheduleOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.name}})}
29437:   function tabbar(){return '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 shadow-sm p-2 flex gap-2 flex-wrap">'+T.map(function(x){return '<button type="button" data-hr-tab="'+x[0]+'" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl font-black text-sm '+(H.tab===x[0]?'bg-indigo-600 text-white':'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50')+'"><i class="fas '+x[2]+' ml-1"></i>'+x[1]+'</button>'}).join('')+'</div>'}
29438:   function employeeMeta(e){return '<div class="space-y-2 text-sm"><div><span class="text-slate-500">القسم:</span> <b>'+esc(e.department_name||e.department||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">الوظيفة:</span> <b>'+esc(e.position_name||e.job_title||e.role||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">الفرع:</span> <b>'+esc(e.branch_name||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">العقد:</span> '+(e.contract_status==='active'?badge('فعال','ok'):badge(e.contract_status||'غير موجود','muted'))+'</div></div>'}

29477:   (function installModalResilience(){
29478:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
29479:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
29480:       if(!ac)return;
29481:       var page=E('rw-page-container');

29486:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
29487:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
29488:       if(!root)return;
29489:       e.preventDefault();
29490:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
29491:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
29492:     });
29493:     try{
29494:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
29495:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
29496:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
29497:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
29498:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
29499:       });
29500:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
29501:     }catch(e){}
29502:   }());

27410: function _exportReportCsv() {
27411:     var resultDiv = byId('report-result');
27412: 
27413:     if (!resultDiv) {
27414:         _showToast('لا يوجد تقرير للتصدير', 'info');
27415:         return;
27416:     }
27417: 
27418:     var table = resultDiv.querySelector('table');
27419: 
27420:     if (!table) {
27421:         _showToast('لا يوجد جدول قابل للتصدير', 'info');
27422:         return;
27423:     }
27424: 
27425:     var rows = table.querySelectorAll('tr');
27426:     var csv = [];
27427: 
27428:     function csvCell(value) {
27429:         var s = String(value == null ? '' : value)
27430:             .replace(/\r?\n|\r/g, ' ')
27431:             .replace(/"/g, '""');
27432: 
27433:         return '"' + s + '"';
27434:     }
27435: 
27436:     for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
27437:         var cells = rows[i].querySelectorAll('th,td');
27438:         var line = [];
27439: 
27440:         for (var j = 0; j < cells.length; j++) {
27441:             line.push(csvCell(cells[j].innerText || ''));
27442:         }
27443: 
27444:         csv.push(line.join(','));
27445:     }
27446: 
27447:     var blob = new Blob(
27448:         ['\uFEFF' + csv.join('\r\n')],
27449:         { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' }
27450:     );
27451: 
27452:     var url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
27453:     var a = document.createElement('a');
27454: 
27455:     a.href = url;
27456:     a.download =
27457:         'rawaea-report-' +
27458:         String(_currentReport || 'report') +
27459:         '-' +
27460:         new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) +
27461:         '.csv';
27462: 
27463:     document.body.appendChild(a);
27464:     a.click();
27465:     document.body.removeChild(a);
27466: 
27467:     URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
27468: }
27469: function _printReport() {
27470:     var resultDiv = byId('report-result');
27471: 
27472:     if (!resultDiv || !resultDiv.innerHTML) {
27473:         _showToast('لا يوجد تقرير للطباعة', 'info');
27474:         return;
27475:     }
27476: 
27477:     var printWindow = window.open('', '_blank');
27478: 
27479:     if (!printWindow) {
27480:         _showToast('الرجاء السماح بالنوافذ المنبثقة', 'warning');
27481:         return;
27482:     }
27483: 
27484:     var html =
27485:         '<!DOCTYPE html>' +
27486:         '<html dir="rtl">' +
27487:         '<head>' +
27488:         '<meta charset="UTF-8">' +
27489:         '<title>تقرير الروائع ERP</title>' +
27490:         '<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Cairo:wght@400;700;900&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">' +
27491:         '<style>' +
27492:         'body{font-family:Cairo,sans-serif;padding:20px;color:#111827}' +
27493:         'table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin-top:15px}' +
27494:         'th,td{border:1px solid #ddd;padding:8px}' +
27495:         'th{background:#f2f2f2;font-weight:800}' +
27496:         '.text-center{text-align:center}' +
27497:         '</style>' +
27498:         '</head>' +
27499:         '<body>' +
27500:         resultDiv.innerHTML +
27501:         '<script>window.onload=function(){window.print();};<\/script>' +
27502:         '</body></html>';
27503: 
27504:     printWindow.document.open();
27505:     printWindow.document.write(html);
27506:     printWindow.document.close();
27507: }

29359: var RW_HR = (function() {
29360:  'use strict';
29361:   var H={tab:'dashboard',actor:null,companyId:null,employees:[],branches:[],channel:null,timer:null,busy:false,ops:{}};
29362:   var T=[
29363:     ['dashboard','لوحة التحكم','fa-chart-pie'],['employees','الموظفون','fa-users'],['organization','الهيكل','fa-sitemap'],
29364:     ['contracts','العقود','fa-file-contract'],['attendance','الحضور','fa-clock'],['leaves','الإجازات','fa-calendar-days'],
29365:     ['requests','الطلبات','fa-list-check'],['advances','السلف','fa-hand-holding-dollar'],['payroll','الرواتب','fa-money-check-dollar'],['documents','المستندات','fa-folder-open']
29366:   ];
29367:   function E(id){return typeof byId==='function'?byId(id):document.getElementById(id)}
29368:   function esc(v){return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/\"/g,'&quot;').replace(/'/g,'&#39;')}
29369:   function num(v){v=Number(v);return isFinite(v)?v:0}
29370:   function money(v){return num(v).toLocaleString('ar-EG',{maximumFractionDigits:2})}
29371:   function date(v){return v?String(v).slice(0,10).split('-').reverse().join('/'):'-'}
29372:   function iso(v){return v?new Date(v).toISOString():null}
29373:   function toast(m,k){if(typeof showToast==='function')return showToast(m,k||'success');if(typeof Swal!=='undefined')return Swal.fire({toast:true,position:'top-end',icon:k||'success',title:m,showConfirmButton:false,timer:2600});alert(m)}
29374:   function safe(el,html){if(!el)return;if(typeof safeHTML==='function')safeHTML(el,html);else el.innerHTML=html}
29375:   function opKey(k){if(!H.ops[k])H.ops[k]='MOTHER-HR:'+k+':'+Date.now()+':'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,10);return H.ops[k]}
29376:   function opClear(k){if(k)delete H.ops[k]}
29377:   async function actor(){var a=await supabase.auth.getUser();if(a.error||!a.data.user)throw Error('جلسة المستخدم غير صالحة');var u=await supabase.from('users').select('id,email,company_id,role,name,status,phone,employee_id,default_branch_id,active_warehouse_role').eq('auth_id',a.data.user.id).maybeSingle();if(u.error)throw u.error;if(!u.data||!u.data.id||!u.data.company_id)throw Error('تعذر تحديد سياق الموظف والشركة');H.actor=u.data;H.companyId=u.data.company_id}
29378:   async function q(view,payload){var r=await supabase.rpc('hr_query',{p_view:view,p_payload:payload||{}});if(r.error)throw r.error;if(!r.data||r.data.success===false)throw Error((r.data&&(r.data.msg||r.data.code))||'فشل قراءة HR');return r.data}
29379:   async function c(command,payload,key){var k=key||('cmd:'+command);var r=await supabase.rpc('hr_command_atomic',{p_command:command,p_payload:payload||{},p_operation_id:opKey(k),p_actor_user_id:H.actor.id,p_actor_email:H.actor.email});if(r.error)throw r.error;if(!r.data||r.data.success===false)throw Error((r.data&&(r.data.msg||r.data.code))||'فشل تنفيذ أمر HR');opClear(k);return r.data}
29380:   function btn(text,action,cls){return '<button type="button" data-hr-action="'+esc(action)+'" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl font-black text-sm '+(cls||'bg-indigo-600 text-white hover:bg-indigo-700')+'">'+esc(text)+'</button>'}
29381:   function badge(text,k){var m={ok:'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-100',warn:'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-100',bad:'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-100',info:'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-100',muted:'bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 border-slate-100'};return '<span class="inline-flex items-center px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full border text-[11px] font-black '+(m[k]||m.muted)+'">'+esc(text)+'</span>'}
29382:   function card(title,sub,body,actions){return '<section class="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-100 shadow-sm overflow-hidden"><div class="px-6 py-5 bg-slate-50/80 border-b flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-center justify-between gap-3"><div><h3 class="font-black text-slate-800">'+esc(title)+'</h3><p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">'+esc(sub||'')+'</p></div><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">'+(actions||'')+'</div></div><div class="p-6">'+body+'</div></section>'}
29383:   function stat(title,value,icon,cls){return '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-5 shadow-sm"><div class="flex items-center justify-between"><div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500 font-bold">'+esc(title)+'</div><div class="text-2xl font-black mt-2">'+esc(value)+'</div></div><div class="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center '+(cls||'bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700')+'"><i class="fas '+icon+'"></i></div></div></div>'}
29384:   function table(headers,rows){if(!rows||!rows.length)return '<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لا توجد بيانات</div>';return '<div class="overflow-auto"><table class="min-w-full text-sm"><thead><tr>'+headers.map(function(h){return '<th class="px-4 py-3 text-right bg-slate-50 text-slate-500 font-black whitespace-nowrap">'+esc(h)+'</th>'}).join('')+'</tr></thead><tbody>'+rows.join('')+'</tbody></table></div>'}
29385:   function tr(cells){return '<tr class="border-t border-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-50/70">'+cells.map(function(x){return '<td class="px-4 py-3 align-top">'+x+'</td>'}).join('')+'</tr>'}
29386:   function field(label,id,value,type,extra){return '<label class="block"><span class="block text-xs font-black text-slate-600 mb-2">'+esc(label)+'</span><input id="'+esc(id)+'" type="'+esc(type||'text')+'" value="'+esc(value==null?'':value)+'" '+(extra||'')+' class="w-full px-4 py-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-200"></label>'}
29387:   function textarea(label,id,value){return '<label class="block"><span class="block text-xs font-black text-slate-600 mb-2">'+esc(label)+'</span><textarea id="'+esc(id)+'" class="w-full px-4 py-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 min-h-[95px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-200">'+esc(value||'')+'</textarea></label>'}
29388:   function select(label,id,list,value){return '<label class="block"><span class="block text-xs font-black text-slate-600 mb-2">'+esc(label)+'</span><select id="'+esc(id)+'" class="w-full px-4 py-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200">'+(list||[]).map(function(x){return '<option value="'+esc(x.value)+'"'+(String(x.value)===String(value==null?'':value)?' selected':'')+'>'+esc(x.label)+'</option>'}).join('')+'</select></label>'}
29389:  function modal(title,body,onSubmit,key){

29395:   document.body.appendChild(r);

29397:   E('rw-hr-cancel').onclick=closeModal;

29400:     if(ac){
29401:       e.preventDefault();
29402:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
29403:     }
29404:   });
29405:   if(onSubmit===null){
29406:     var f=E('rw-hr-form');

29408:   }else{
29409:     E('rw-hr-form').onsubmit=async function(e){
29410:       e.preventDefault();

29412:       try{

29415:           save.textContent='جارٍ الحفظ…';
29416:         }
29417:         await onSubmit(key||'form:'+Date.now());

29420:         if(save){

29422:           save.textContent='حفظ';
29423:         }
29424:       }
29425:     };
29426:   }
29427: }

29477:   (function installModalResilience(){
29478:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
29479:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
29480:       if(!ac)return;
29481:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
29482:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
29483:       e.preventDefault();
29484:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
29485:     },true);
29486:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
29487:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
29488:       if(!root)return;
29489:       e.preventDefault();
29490:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
29491:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
29492:     });
29493:     try{
29494:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
29495:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
29496:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
29497:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
29498:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
29499:       });
29500:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
29501:     }catch(e){}
29502:   }());

29493:     try{

29499:       });
29500:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
29501:     }catch(e){}
29502:   }());

29510: var RW_CRM = (function() {
29511:     'use strict';
29512: 
29513:     var state = {
29514:         customers: [],
29515:         assignees: [],
29516:         kpi: {},
29517:         search: '',
29518:         activeOnly: false,
29519:         searchTimer: null
29520:     };
29521: 
29522:     function _esc(s) {
29523:         return String(s == null ? '' : s)
29524:             .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
29525:             .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
29526:             .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
29527:             .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
29528:             .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
29529:     }
29530: 
29531:     function _fmtNum(n) {
29532:         return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('ar-EG');
29533:     }
29534: 
29535:     function _fmtMoney(n) {
29536:         return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('ar-EG') + ' EGP';
29537:     }
29538: 
29539:     function _today() {
29540:         var d = new Date();
29541:         var local = new Date(d.getTime() - d.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000);
29542:         return local.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
29543:     }
29544: 
29545:     function _statusLabel(s) {
29546:         var map = {
29547:             Open: 'مفتوحة',
29548:             'معلقة': 'معلقة',
29549:             completed: 'مكتملة',
29550:             'مكتملة': 'مكتملة',
29551:             cancelled: 'ملغاة',
29552:             'ملغاة': 'ملغاة'
29553:         };
29554:         return map[s] || s || 'غير محددة';
29555:     }
29556: 
29557:     function _statusClass(s) {
29558:         if (s === 'completed' || s === 'مكتملة') return 'bg-green-100 text-green-700';
29559:         if (s === 'cancelled' || s === 'ملغاة') return 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600';
29560:         return 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700';
29561:     }
29562: 
29563:     function _assignedLabel(customer) {
29564:         var rows = Array.isArray(customer && customer.assigned_to) ? customer.assigned_to : [];
29565:         if (!rows.length) return 'غير مسند';
29566:         var active = rows.filter(function(x) { return x && x.active !== false; });
29567:         if (!active.length) active = rows;
29568:         return active.slice(0, 2).map(function(x) {
29569:             return x.name || x.email || '—';
29570:         }).join('، ') + (active.length > 2 ? ' +' + (active.length - 2) : '');
29571:     }
29572: 
29573:     async function _loadDirectory() {
29574:         var res = await supabase.rpc('crm_customer_directory', {
29575:             p_search: state.search || null,
29576:             p_active_only: state.activeOnly,
29577:             p_limit: 200,
29578:             p_offset: 0
29579:         });
29580:         if (res.error) throw res.error;
29581: 
29582:         var payload = res.data || {};
29583:         state.customers = Array.isArray(payload.customers) ? payload.customers : [];
29584:         state.assignees = Array.isArray(payload.assignees) ? payload.assignees : [];
29585:         state.kpi = payload.kpi || {};
29586:         return payload;
29587:     }
29588: 
29589:     function _renderKpis() {
29590:         var k = state.kpi || {};
29591:         return '<div class="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-6 gap-4">' +
29592:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">إجمالي العملاء</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-indigo-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.total_customers) + '</div></div>' +
29593:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">عملاء نشطون</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-green-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.active_customers) + '</div></div>' +
29594:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">ذمم مسجلة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-red-600 mt-2">' + _fmtMoney(k.master_debt_total) + '</div></div>' +
29595:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">متابعات مفتوحة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-amber-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.open_followups) + '</div></div>' +
29596:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">متأخرة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-rose-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.overdue_followups) + '</div></div>' +
29597:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">مستحقة اليوم</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-blue-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.due_today) + '</div></div>' +
29598:         '</div>';
29599:     }
29600: 
29601:     function _renderTable() {
29602:         if (!state.customers.length) {
29603:             return '<div class="text-center py-16 text-gray-400"><div class="text-5xl mb-3">👥</div><div class="font-black text-lg">لا توجد عملاء مطابقون</div><div class="text-sm mt-2">غيّر البحث أو الفلاتر ثم أعد المحاولة.</div></div>';
29604:         }
29605: 
29606:         var html = '<div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-sm">' +
29607:             '<thead class="bg-slate-50"><tr>' +
29608:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">العميل</th>' +
29609:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">التواصل</th>' +
29610:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">التصنيف</th>' +
29611:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">المبيعات</th>' +
29612:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">الأوردرات</th>' +
29613:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">المتابعة القادمة</th>' +
29614:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">المسؤول</th>' +
29615:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">الإجراء</th>' +
29616:             '</tr></thead><tbody>';
29617: 
29618:         for (var i = 0; i < state.customers.length; i++) {
29619:             var c = state.customers[i] || {};
29620:             var overdue = Number(c.overdue_followups || 0) > 0;
29621:             var next = c.next_followup_date ? String(c.next_followup_date) : '—';
29622: 
29623:             html += '<tr class="border-b hover:bg-slate-50">' +
29624:                 '<td class="p-3"><div class="font-black">' + _esc(c.name) + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.customer_code) + '</div></td>' +
29625:                 '<td class="p-3"><div>' + _esc(c.phone || '—') + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.area || '—') + '</div></td>' +
29626:                 '<td class="p-3"><div class="font-bold">' + _esc(c.customer_type || '—') + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.payment_type || '—') + '</div></td>' +
29627:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center font-black">' + _fmtMoney(c.sales_total) + '</td>' +
29628:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center font-black">' + _fmtNum(c.order_count) + '</td>' +
29629:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center"><span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-black ' + (overdue ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700') + '">' + _esc(next) + '</span></td>' +
29630:                 '<td class="p-3">' + _esc(_assignedLabel(c)) + '</td>' +
29631:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center"><button type="button" data-crm-open360="' + _esc(c.id) + '" class="px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-xl font-black">متابعة</button></td>' +
29632:             '</tr>';
29633:         }
29634: 
29635:         return html + '</tbody></table></div>';
29636:     }
29637: 
29638:     function _bindDirectory() {
29639:         var search = byId('crm-search');
29640:         if (search) {
29641:             search.value = state.search;
29642:             search.addEventListener('input', function() {
29643:                 state.search = search.value.trim();
29644:                 clearTimeout(state.searchTimer);
29645:                 state.searchTimer = setTimeout(function() {
29646:                     _loadDirectory().then(function() {
29647:                         safeHTML(byId('crm-customers-list'), _renderTable());
29648:                         _bindOpen360();
29649:                         safeHTML(byId('crm-kpis'), _renderKpis());
29650:                     }).catch(function(e) {
29651:                         showToast(e.message || 'فشل البحث', 'error');
29652:                     });
29653:                 }, 250);
29654:             });
29655:         }

29476:   // Resilience layer: modal actions work outside the page-container, async form errors become visible, and 360 is truly read-only.
29477:   (function installModalResilience(){
29478:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
29479:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
29480:       if(!ac)return;
29481:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
29482:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
29483:       e.preventDefault();
29484:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
29485:     },true);
29486:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
29487:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
29488:       if(!root)return;
29489:       e.preventDefault();
29490:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
29491:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
29492:     });
29493:     try{
29494:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
29495:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
29496:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
29497:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
29498:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
29499:       });
29500:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
29501:     }catch(e){}
29502:   }());
